Cloud Run workloads' own requests need Knative's queue-proxy metrics, which are off (#181). Storage (#513): each request is counted and recorded in `/admin/events` by API method (`storage.objects.insert`, `xml.PUT`), with its bucket, object, status and duration, and never its query string or body, so no upload_id, token, signature or secret (`TestStorageEventsRecorded`, `TestStorageEventsRedactUploadIDs`, `TestMetricsStorageIsMeasured`). The server also keeps its last 1000 calls at `GET /_cloudburrow/events?after=N`, for the CLI to scrape from the in-cluster server (#514). |
| **Traffic splitting** | **Not supported** | A Cloud Run service's page shows where its traffic goes, read from the service, but nothing changes the split. Detail screens exist (`TestDetailAddressesAResourcePath`, `TestDetailScreensHaveTabs`). |
| Pagination | **Verified** | Tables page, with rows per page and a range out of the filtered total (`TestTablesPaginate`). Cloud SQL, Firestore, Datastore and Bigtable listings continue past their first page with a cursor, and a cursor the provider did not issue is refused rather than read as the end (`TestEveryPagedListingRefusesACursorItDidNotIssue`). |
| **Bucket listing is not scoped by project** | **Inherited limitation** | The storage backend accepts the project parameter and returns every bucket. The screen **says so** rather than presenting the rows under a project heading. |
| Console walkthrough on a fresh stack | **Verified** | [console-verification.md](console-verification.md): the walkthrough is now `test/browser`, run in headless Chrome against the compat storage shard's instance in CI (#594): landmarks and the LOCAL badge, the theme switch, a bucket created through the form, the error card under an injected fault, a row opened by keyboard, and no request off loopback; since #700 also the four viewport widths, dialog focus and Escape, and the Cloud KMS, Cloud Scheduler, Subscriptions and Cloud Run edit screens, the KMS and Run tests against the run shard's instance. Focus indicators and colour contrast are no longer walkthrough claims: `TestOutlineSuppressionHasAFocusVisibleRule` and `TestTokensMeetContrast` assert them from the stylesheet. |
| Form validation actually runs in a browser | **Verified** | It did **not** before this walkthrough. An HTML `pattern` is compiled with the RegExp `v` flag, where a literal `-` in a character class must be escaped; an unescaped one makes the pattern invalid and the browser then **silently skips validation**. Every create form had shipped that way. A regression test now compiles each shipped pattern under the `v` rule, and `TestCreateBucketThroughTheForm` (`test/browser`) submits a name the bucket pattern rejects in headless Chrome and asserts nothing is sent before creating a valid bucket through the same form; with the `-` unescaped it fails (#594). The regression test covered only the first four products' forms, so the Cloud KMS key ring and key, Cloud Scheduler job and Secret Manager secret patterns shipped the same way; `TestKMSCreateKeyRingThroughTheForm` found the first in Chrome's error log, and the unit test now reads every product's create form (#700). |
| Subscription creation from the UI | **Verified** | From a topic's page; see the Pub/Sub actions row above (`TestConsolePubSubActions`). |
| Visual regression against reference fixtures | **Not possible today** | No reference screenshots exist, so there is nothing to diff against — a threshold measured against our own output would only prove the console still looks like itself. This is what keeps visual fidelity at `Partial`. |
| Model catalogue screen | **Verified** | Shows the real catalogue with publisher, access, modality, licence, runtime and a **per-model** reason it is unavailable — the reasons differ, and one blanket message would hide that. |
| Generation playground | **Verified** | Offered only when local AI is configured, and relayed to the same API path the SDK calls (`TestPlaygroundGeneratesThroughTheRealAPIPath`); see [Console playground](#console-playground). An embedding playground is **not implemented** ([playground.md](playground.md) §7); custom prediction requests are the Online prediction page ([playground.md](playground.md) §8). |
| Gemma results labelled as Gemini | **Cannot occur** | The playground shows the model and its publisher, and says, where the output appears, that a community conversion's output is not a Gemini result (`TestPlaygroundReportsReadinessFromALiveProbe`). |

**Visual fidelity and API compatibility are separate claims with separate evidence**, and
neither implies the other. A screen that renders correctly proves nothing about the API
behind it, and a verified API proves nothing about the screen.

## Cross-cutting

| Concern | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Google-style error bodies and gRPC status codes | **Verified** | `internal/apierror`: one cause renders a consistent gRPC code and HTTP status, plus the JSON envelope a Google client parses. Used by Cloud Tasks, Secret Manager, Cloud KMS, Cloud Scheduler, Cloud Logging, Resource Manager, Cloud Storage and the Cloud Run v2 adapter. |
| Pagination with deterministic ordering | **Verified** | `internal/paging`: full-walk coverage proves no duplicates or gaps; invalid and cross-listing tokens are rejected. Used by the list methods of Cloud Tasks, Secret Manager, Cloud KMS, Cloud Scheduler, Cloud Logging, Resource Manager and the Cloud Run v2 adapter. |
| Long-running operations | **Verified** | `internal/lro`: pending, succeeded and failed are all reachable and terminal states are final. Used by the Cloud Run v2 adapter's operations. |
| Project isolation | **Verified** | `TestPubSubProjectIsolation`; `internal/resource` makes it structural — identical IDs in different projects or locations produce different keys by construction. |
| REST and gRPC transport | **Verified** | `internal/transport`: unknown REST paths return a Google 404 envelope and unknown gRPC methods return `Unimplemented`; request sizes are bounded and unknown JSON fields rejected. Cloud Tasks, Secret Manager, Cloud KMS, Cloud Scheduler, Cloud Logging, Resource Manager and the Cloud Run v2 adapter are served on it. |
| Background workers registered after startup | **Verified** | A worker registered after `Start` used to sit in the list and never run, because `Start` snapshotted the list once. **Cloud Tasks dispatch was wired into `up` and never dispatched anything.** Found while building the Logs Explorer: a deliberately failing task showed `dispatches=0` after minutes. Now started immediately, sharing the coordinator's lifetime. |
| Scheduling, retry and backoff over an injected clock | **Verified** | `internal/sched`: retry timing is proven by advancing virtual time, never by sleeping. Concurrent duplicate attempts are prevented, and shutdown drains in-flight work. |
| Metadata storage, memory and durable modes | **Verified** | `internal/store`: both modes share one test body; durable state survives restart, a second instance is refused, a failed commit leaves state unchanged, and unsafe keys are rejected. |
| Resource-name parsing and traversal safety | **Verified** | `internal/resource` rejects `..`, encoded separators, NUL and path separators in IDs. |
| Installation and release packaging | Partial | `docs/install.md`, `docs/status.md` and a release workflow that builds and checksums four platforms. v0.1.0 was published by it. The post-publish smoke install (#603) was added after v0.1.0 and has not run on a release yet. |
| Reset / seed / event inspection | Partial | `internal/admin`, served on the loopback-only control port and refused on service ports. **Reset covers Cloud Tasks, Cloud Storage (buckets, objects and notification configurations), Pub/Sub, Secret Manager and Cloud KMS**, narrowable with `service=` and `project=` (`TestAProjectResetClearsOnlyThatProject`). Storage buckets record their project (#510), so a project-scoped reset clears that project's buckets only (`TestAProjectResetIncludingStorage`). The storage server's reset clears its store directly: live, noncurrent and soft-deleted objects, soft-deleted buckets, upload sessions, rewrite tokens, notification configurations and undelivered events, multipart uploads, IAM policies and HMAC keys, with no API delete and so no soft delete or notification (`TestAStorageResetEmptiesEveryBucketOnBuiltin`, `TestAStorageProjectResetOnBuiltin`). A Pub/Sub reset deletes a project's subscriptions, snapshots, topics and, since #889, schemas (`TestPubSubResetDeletesSchemas`: a schema is `NOT_FOUND` after its project's reset, its ID can be created again, and another project's schema is kept; unit `TestPubSubResetDeletesSchemas`). Pub/Sub state in a project CloudBurrow has never seen survives a full reset. **Seed covers Cloud Tasks, Cloud Storage, Pub/Sub, Secret Manager and Cloud Scheduler** from one document validated whole before anything is created (`TestOneSeedDocumentIsReadBackByTheOfficialSDKs`, `TestAnInvalidSeedDocumentSeedsNothing`, `TestSchedulerSeedIsReadBackByTheOfficialClient`). Pub/Sub fields the emulator is not known to honour are refused by name. Pub/Sub **schemas** (Avro only) and a topic's **`schemaSettings`** are seeded since #890: `TestPubSubSeedSchemaAndBoundTopic` seeds a schema and a topic bound to it, both read back by the official clients, a conforming message is published and a non-conforming one refused `INVALID_ARGUMENT`, a repeat is a 409 and `ifNotExists` skips it, and a `PROTOCOL_BUFFER` schema is refused with 400 before anything is seeded (unit `TestPubSubSeedCreatesSchemasAndBindsTopics`, `TestPubSubSeedValidatesSchemasFirst`). A subscription's **`expirationPolicy`, `messageRetentionDuration` and `retainAckedMessages`** are seeded since #899: `TestPubSubSeedExpirationAndRetention` seeds a 1-day ttl with a 1-hour retention and acknowledged messages kept, and a policy without a ttl, each read back by the official client; the 1-day one is gone 25 hours on by the front's clock and the other kept; a 12-hour ttl, and a 1-day ttl under the default 7-day retention, are refused 400 before anything is seeded, by the front's own check (unit `TestPubSubSeedSetsExpirationAndRetention`). Bucket labels, location and storage class are seeded and kept (`TestStorageSeedAcceptsLabels`, #503). **Events record every API call on the ports CloudBurrow serves itself** — Cloud Tasks (gRPC and JSON, #591), Secret Manager (gRPC and JSON), the Cloud Run v2 adapter (gRPC and JSON, #591), Cloud KMS, Cloud Scheduler and Cloud Logging (gRPC and JSON, #591), and Cloud Storage, whose calls the CLI scrapes from the in-cluster server (#513, #514) — with method, resource, project, canonical status code and duration, and never a payload or query string (`TestSecretManagerCallsAreRecordedWithoutTheirPayloads`). **Pub/Sub and the opt-in emulators are not recorded**: their traffic goes over a raw port-forward to an upstream process that CloudBurrow does not intercept. |
| Go SDK compatibility harness | **Verified** | `test/compat`. Refuses non-loopback endpoints and fails outright if cloud credentials are present in the environment. |
| Local cluster lifecycle (up/status/stop/reset/delete) | **Verified** | `internal/cluster` integration tests. |
| Workstation preflight (`cloudburrow doctor`) | **Verified** | `internal/doctor`: binaries, daemon reachability, memory, CPUs, disk and every port `up` would bind, each with a remedy. Only genuine blockers exit non-zero; an unmeasurable check reports `unknown`, never `ok`. On macOS, and on Linux when the daemon is in a VM (Docker Desktop for Linux), the disk figure is the host volume backing the VM disk, and says so. It names the container engine from `docker info` and warns on rootless and non-Desktop VM engines ([install.md](install.md#container-engines), #712). Windows is not built ([install.md](install.md#prerequisites)). |
| Diagnostics bundle (`cloudburrow diagnose`) | **Verified** | `test/compat/diagnose_test.go` creates a secret with a known payload, runs `diagnose` against the CI instance and searches every file of the bundle for that payload, the ADC fixture's private key and the kubeconfig's client key; none may appear. A stopped instance is covered by a unit test. Redaction is pattern-based (`console.Redact`) plus removal of pod env values; a credential an application logs in a form those patterns do not recognise is not caught, so read a bundle before sharing it. |
| Cluster ownership isolation | **Verified** | Prefix enforced at construction and re-checked on delete; namespace reset requires `cloudburrow.dev/owned=true`. |
| Python SDK compatibility harness | **Verified** | `test/compat-python`: pytest against the official Python clients, pinned by hash in `requirements.lock`, run by `make compat-python` in compat CI. See [Python client libraries](#python-client-libraries). |
| Node.js SDK compatibility harness | **Verified** | `test/compat-node`: `node:test` against the official Node clients, pinned with integrity hashes in `package-lock.json`, run by `make compat-node` in compat CI's `storage` and `emulators` shards, where every test passed with none skipped (#589). See [Node.js client libraries](#nodejs-client-libraries). |
| Java SDK support | Untested, no plan | No Java test exists and none is planned (#718), so CloudBurrow makes no Java claim; [status.md](status.md) lists Java as Untested. The endpoint each Java client needs has not been checked against client source, so none is documented. |
